# Wordocious content audit — puzzle answers & word lists

_Audit date 2026-10-06 · branch `cloud/content-audit` · report only: no bank, app code or version number was changed._

Machine-readable copy of every flag: [`content-audit.json`](content-audit.json). Guard-test proposals (not wired in): [`docs/content-audit/proposals/`](docs/content-audit/proposals/).

## TL;DR

- **1788 flags** across 13 content sources (181 high, 680 medium, 927 low). 1699 are on future/unseen content and changeable; 89 sit on already-served content and are listed as frozen.
- **Profanity already in a future bank:** Muddle daily `md-ikz7lk` (2027-01-29) has the scramble word **F*******, and the Kwanzaa Muddle `md-9guhqs` has **S*******. The answer-pool swap batch 2 removed these words from the Classic pools, but the Muddle bank had already been built from the unswapped pool, and no guard checks Muddle words against the profanity lists.
- **Slurs and sexual words outside the Classic pools:** Crossword clues *"A C**** in the ____"* (2 puzzles) and *"A C*** and bull ____"* (2). In Hubbub's scored lists: H***, R*******, B****, H****, V******* and B****** (the last two are their puzzles' only pangram). On the Letter Ladder accept list: B****, H****, S****, S****, N****, Q**** and G****.
- **Answer pools:** 5-letter 33 flags (TRUMP, P****, S****, RANDY, B****, plus the 7 words awaiting a founder call). 6-letter 164 flags (R*****, N*****, V*****, J*****, STONED; **TOGGLE is a live Six answer**). 7-letter 97 flags (R******, TALIBAN, P******, ENQUIRE/ENQUIRY, RUBBISH, NETBALL). Each has a same-length replacement that passes the batch rules, for a proposed **swap batch 4**.
- **British content is concentrated in idioms:** Crossword and Codebreaker fill-in-the-blank sayings ("Pull your socks up" ×5, "Mind the gap" ×4, "Bob's your uncle", "Pigs might fly", "storm in a teacup", "stable door…bolted", "An Englishman's home…"), Muddle captions ("lollipop man", HIGHLY STRUNG, LOST PROPERTY, TRUNK CALL), and Hubbub's TONNE ×13, TORY ×9, PENCE ×9 and GIRO ×8.
- **Duplicates:** Kindred's "Sound like numbers: WON TOO FORE ATE" group appears word-for-word in **6 puzzles**, and three Kindred puzzles are near-copies of others. Codebreaker has 4 sayings twice. Eight ProperNoundle answers are both in the daily rotation and the holiday file; Sacagawea is the holiday puzzle on **2026-10-12**, three days after the release cutover. Crossword has about 60 grids that repeat a word stem (EGG/EGGS, TREE/TREES…).
- The three platform copies of every bundled bank are **byte-identical** (web = iOS = Android), so each fix is one edit followed by the usual copy step.

## Rules respected

- **CONTENT_RELEASE_DATE = 2026-10-09** (`apps/web/scripts/content-release-date.mjs`). Bank dailies dated 2026-09-23…2026-10-08 (index 0–15) are already bundled in the store builds. They are marked **frozen**: flagged where wrong, but no replacement is proposed.
- **Answer swaps** (`packages/core/src/solution-swaps.ts`): batch 1 started 2026-10-05; batches 2 and 3 start 2026-11-16. Words those batches already swap out are not re-flagged. Every pool flag is assessed **as dealt after batch 3**, and every replacement is checked against the batch rules: valid guess, not a current or legacy answer, not an earlier swap replacement, not blocklisted, not British. The tables are append-only (`SOLUTION_SWAPS*` must never grow), so the replacements are proposed as a **new batch 4** with its own future cutover. Dates already dealt keep their words.
- **Already decided:** the `ALLOW` entries in `content-american.test.ts` (PLOUGH "spelled the British way" ×2, TROUSERS ×3, Kindred GREY in ___hound), its OFF_THEME/OBSCURE lists, its empty `PENDING`, and `answer-pool-hygiene.test.ts`. The `PENDING_FOUNDER_CALL` words (BOOZE VOMIT DRUNK URINE O**** NAKED SLAVE SLANT DAMNED DAMNING SLAVERY COLORED HOSPICE DRUNKEN) are listed with replacements and marked as awaiting the founder's call.
- Proposals only. Nothing in any bank was changed.

## Where the content lives (three platform copies)

Each file sits under the same name in three places: **web** `apps/web/data/` (canonical; `apps/server` reads it directly), **iOS** `apps/ios/Wordocious/Resources/`, and **Android** `apps/android/core/src/main/resources/data/` (word-definitions.json: `apps/android/app/src/main/assets/`).
Test copies are in `apps/ios/Tests/Fixtures/` and `apps/android/core/src/test/resources/fixtures/`; the answer pools have them, the ProperNoundle files do not.

| Game(s) | Bundled file(s) | Build / repair script |
|---|---|---|
| Classic, QuadWord, OctoWord, Succession, Deliverance, Gauntlet, daily VS, bots, Unlimited, Word of the Day | solutions.json (+ solutions-legacy.json, allowed.json) | swaps: packages/core/src/solution-swaps.ts (+ SolutionSwaps.swift / .kt mirrors) |
| Six / Seven | solutions-6.json, solutions-7.json (+ -legacy, allowed-6/7.json) | same swap tables |
| Crosswordocious | crossword-puzzles.json | scripts/crossword/build-bank.mjs |
| Kindred | groups-puzzles.json | scripts/groups/build-bank.mjs |
| Spyglass | wordsearch-puzzles.json (theme pools: wordsearch-themes.json, web-only build source) | scripts/wordsearch/repair-future.mjs |
| Muddle | scramble-puzzles.json | scripts/muddle/build-bank.mjs |
| Codebreaker | cryptogram-puzzles.json | scripts/cryptogram/build-bank.mjs |
| Hubbub | hub-puzzles.json | scripts/hub/repair-future.mjs |
| Letter Ladder | ladder-puzzles.json, ladder-words.json (accept list) | scripts/ladder/repair-future.mjs, build-ladder-words.mjs |
| ProperNoundle | propernoundle-puzzles.json, propernoundle-holidays.json | scripts/validate-propernoundle.mjs |
| Holiday calendar (all banks) | holiday-days.json | scripts/holidays/gen-holiday-days.mjs |
| Word of the Day definitions | word-definitions.json | scripts/gen-word-defs.mjs |

Other notes:

- `apps/server` reads `apps/web/data` directly for VS and ProperNoundle.
- Every bundled copy was md5-checked and all three platforms are identical (`word-list-sync.test.ts` pins the copies).
- **Starsweep** and **Sudocious** are number/star logic puzzles with no word content, so there is nothing to audit.
- The **holiday answer and holiday WotD lists** (`apps/web/scripts/holidays/holiday-answers.json`, `holiday-wotd.json`) are validated but **not wired into any runtime yet**. They are audited so they ship clean.
- **VS bots** use the same answer pools and have no word list of their own.
- **Word of the Day** is `solutions.json[localDay % n]`, read through the swap batches and skipping blocklisted and definition-less words. For the 5-letter flags the JSON gives the next WotD date.

Status of the content reviewed. *Future* means a daily on or after 2026-10-09. *Unlimited* is the Unlimited pool. *Holiday* is a holiday-only entry. *Never-dated* is a daily that a holiday displaces. *Frozen* means already bundled or served.

| Bank | future | unlimited | holiday | never-dated | frozen |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Crosswordocious | 358 | 82 | 84 | 36 | 16 |
| Kindred | 332 | 80 | 56 | 32 | 16 |
| Spyglass | 384 | 150 | 0 | 0 | 16 |
| Muddle | 317 | 21 | 56 | 32 | 16 |
| Codebreaker | 324 | 60 | 84 | 32 | 16 |
| Hubbub | 384 | 200 | 0 | 0 | 16 |
| Letter Ladder | 384 | 200 | 0 | 0 | 16 |
| ProperNoundle | 26 | 8 | 56 | 0 | 577 |

ProperNoundle rotates forever: day N plays category N mod 7, and the index in that category advances every 7 days and wraps. So 577 of its entries have already been served and will come round again, and editing one in place would also change its archived past dates.

**4. ProperNoundle**: 577 of 669 rotation entries have been served since the 2024-01-01 epoch (the list of flagged ones is in table 2). Entries over 15 letters (58) are never served.

## How to apply the proposals (when approved)

1. **Answer pools.** Add `SOLUTION_SWAPS_4` with its own `SOLUTION_SWAP_4_CUTOVER_DATE`, set after the next coordinated web + iOS + Android release carrying the table. Mirror it in `SolutionSwaps.swift` and `SolutionSwaps.kt`, extend `solutionSwapBatchesFor`, and pin it in the solution-swaps tests. Keys are the dealt words in this report. They are already batch-3-aware: e.g. CRUMPET and DUVET are gone, and no replacement collides with batches 1–3.
2. **Spyglass.** Edit the theme pools in `wordsearch-themes.json`, then run `scripts/wordsearch/repair-future.mjs` (dailies from CONTENT_RELEASE_DATE plus Unlimited only).
3. **Hubbub.** Run `scripts/hub/repair-future.mjs` after extending its British/blocked lists, or move the words to the accepted list by hand and recompute `max`.
4. **Letter Ladder.** Drop the flagged words from `build-ladder-words.mjs`'s source, rebuild `ladder-words.json`, then run `scripts/ladder/repair-future.mjs`. The path replacements listed above are same-par and verified on the current list.
5. **Crossword, Kindred, Muddle, Codebreaker, ProperNoundle.** Make in-place edits on the future, Unlimited and holiday entries named above, keeping ids. Crossword answer swaps keep every crossing letter; the clue in the note goes with each. Muddle swaps keep the circled letters, with new indexes in the note.
6. Copy each changed bank to the iOS (`Resources` + `Tests/Fixtures`) and Android bundles, run `content-american.test.ts`, `word-list-sync.test.ts` and `bank-runway.test.ts`, and ship before the first affected date. The earliest affected dates are 2026-10-12 (Sacagawea holiday duplicate, Indigenous Peoples' Day) and Muddle/Crossword dailies from 2026-10-09.

## Guard-test proposals (not wired in)

See `docs/content-audit/proposals/README.md`. In short:

- an extended British vocabulary list (`brit-words-extended.txt`)
- a profanity/slur check over **every** More Games bank (Muddle words, Hubbub required words, ladder-words, crossword clues), which would have caught F***** and S*****
- a cross-puzzle duplicate-group check for Kindred, and a duplicate-saying check for Codebreaker
- a crossword stem-repeat check
- a ProperNoundle rotation-vs-holiday duplicate check
- a proposed `OBSCURE_WORDS` extension

All of it is in one runnable, dependency-free script, `content-audit-guards.proposal.mjs`, which prints what it would fail on today.

## Method

- Every bundled bank was extracted with its serving status, using the real date math: the bank epoch 2026-09-23, holiday displacement from `holiday-days.json`, the ProperNoundle category rotation, and Classic-family deck deals simulated through `packages/core` seed + dictionary code to 2035 (`generateSolutionsFromSeed*` with all swap batches).
- **Mechanical pass.** The guard's own lists (the 1,678 British spellings from spelling-copy, BRIT_WORDS, OBSCURE_WORDS), an extended British list, the repo blocklists (offensive, profanity, taste, manual) and word frequency (wordfreq zipf), plus duplicate detection.
- **Read-through.** Every word, clue, label, caption and saying was read by a reviewer with the game's rules. Replacements were machine-validated:
  - pool swaps pass the batch rules
  - crossword swaps match the crossing pattern
  - ladder paths are on the accept list and equal par
  - Muddle swaps keep the circled letters
  - Codebreaker sayings are within ±20% of the original length and not already in the bank
- Lead review dropped 6 reviewer flags as false positives (cw-kmdxa2 3D MUM: "Mum's the word" is standard American usage; cw-2ils5o 11A MUM: "Mum's the word" is standard American usage; pool6 SLEDGE: ordinary American usage; pool6 WINGER: ordinary American usage; pool6 TARMAC: ordinary American usage; pool6 REGENT: ordinary American usage) and corrected one crossword replacement that broke a crossing.
- Calibration: words a newspaper reader knows (ALOOF, BRAISE, CHEMIST, CARAVAN, TROLLEY, PINT, MATE…) were **not** flagged. Low severity marks taste calls for the founder.
